# Open questions for atlas approval

All questions below are part of the review material; they do not request a new approval flow for this documentation task. The context map has no named owners. Product should nominate an accountable owner for every context before calling this the approved source of truth.

## Product / architecture

1. Is the context map or conformance manifest authoritative for Chessboard? Should Whole product be an independent context or a composition of existing ones? [Affected product map](atlas.yaml) and [partition conflict](spec-conflicts.md#context-partition).
2. How should this requested nested atlas be admitted alongside the flat existing specs and the markdown naming rule? Existing specs and validators have not been modified.
3. Who owns each context and who approves a state as confirmed? Every chart currently names its context owner without inventing a person.

## profile

Owner: Identity owner (not named in specs).

**Product / legal:** What message and recovery remain available on the deletion receipt after the account has signed out? What legal purpose, retention end condition and export deadline are promised in every jurisdiction? Confirm the thirty-day ordinary-erasure, twenty-four-hour preparation and seven-day export bounds.

### account-privacy

**Product:** Does [account-privacy](contexts/profile/flows/account-privacy/machine.yaml) describe the intended user-visible outcomes and seed-dependent branches? 9 states are assumed; a Gherkin mapping or a successful Quint trace alone does not settle layout, all hidden preconditions or complete code agreement.

**Design / engineering:** Which stable production UI signals recognize these states? [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fprofile%2Fflows%2Faccount-privacy&state=Told%20a%20connection%20is%20required) Told a connection is required, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fprofile%2Fflows%2Faccount-privacy&state=Deleting%20the%20account) Deleting the account, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fprofile%2Fflows%2Faccount-privacy&state=Told%20deletion%20failed) Told deletion failed, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fprofile%2Fflows%2Faccount-privacy&state=Preparing%20a%20personal-data%20export) Preparing a personal-data export, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fprofile%2Fflows%2Faccount-privacy&state=Personal-data%20export%20ready) Personal-data export ready, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fprofile%2Fflows%2Faccount-privacy&state=Told%20export%20failed) Told export failed, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fprofile%2Fflows%2Faccount-privacy&state=Personal-data%20export%20expired) Personal-data export expired, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fprofile%2Fflows%2Faccount-privacy&state=Downloading%20own%20export) Downloading own export. Add state-specific test IDs only in later implementation.

**Both context owners:** Confirm each reception transition and its in-progress modal/checkout reaction in [interactions.md](interactions.md); do not default an unreviewed handler to a silent self-loop.

## feedback

[View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Viewing%20Journeys%20with%20current%20information) Viewing Journeys with current information, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20account-bound%20choices%20become%20available%20only%20where%20the%20current%20permission%20decision%20allows%20them) Seeing account-bound choices become available only where the current permission decision allows them, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20account-only%20choices%20are%20denied%20and%20the%20person%20sees%20the%20current%20identity) Seeing account-only choices are denied and the person sees the current identity, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20the%20person%E2%80%99s%20current%20name%20replaces%20their%20earlier%20name%20on%20affected%20public%20surfaces) Seeing the person’s current name replaces their earlier name on affected public surfaces, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20the%20next%20permission%20decision%20recognizes%20paid%20access) Seeing the next permission decision recognizes paid access, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20the%20next%20permission%20decision%20recognizes%20free%20limits%3B%20an%20already%20started%20puzzle%20may%20finish) Seeing the next permission decision recognizes free limits; an already started puzzle may finish, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20an%20open%20space%20becomes%20visible%20only%20to%20people%20entitled%20to%20find%20it) Seeing an open space becomes visible only to people entitled to find it, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20current%20presence%20changes%20for%20people%20who%20can%20view%20that%20space) Seeing current presence changes for people who can view that space, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20the%20person%20no%20longer%20appears%20as%20present%20in%20that%20space) Seeing the person no longer appears as present in that space, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20the%20ended%20space%20is%20removed%20from%20live%20choices%20and%20an%20open%20participant%20view%20shows%20it%20ended) Seeing the ended space is removed from live choices and an open participant view shows it ended, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20the%20new%20friendship%20appears%20and%20may%20make%20a%20friend%E2%80%99s%20open%20space%20discoverable) Seeing the new friendship appears and may make a friend’s open space discoverable, [View state](index.html#chart=contexts%2Fjourneys%2Fflows%2Fpublished-event-reactions&state=Seeing%20the%20relationship%20and%20any%20friend-derived%20discoverability%20are%20recomputed) Seeing the relationship and any friend-derived discoverability are recomputed. Add state-specific test IDs only in later implementation.

**Both context owners:** Confirm each reception transition and its in-progress modal/checkout reaction in [interactions.md](interactions.md); do not default an unreviewed handler to a silent self-loop.

## Coverage priority

Review Billing together with Identity deletion first: incorrect money/access and ongoing charges after deletion are the highest risks. Then review Chess/Spaces and Matchmaking for the core promise, then Friends and developer evidence surfaces. Static or calculation-only exclusions may be approved without adding a screen chart.

## Missing boundary contracts — both owners decide

- **Spaces + Friends / product and engineering:** Which published fact tells another context that a space was renamed? `SpaceRenamed` exists in code but not in the published SpaceEvent variants. Affected: space-rename flows, friend-live-space flows and every open lobby showing the old title.
- **Friends + Chess and Matchmaking / product and privacy:** Which public fact withdraws an invitation or opponent eligibility when a stranger is blocked? `BlockChanged` is an internal outbox message, and a block must never tell the blocked person its private cause. Affected: Custom Game invitation/configuration and active opponent searches.
- **Identity + every displaying context / design and engineering:** How does an avatar change reach an open list or profile without a documented published avatar event? Affected: account/profile edits, friends lists and space presence.
- **Billing + Spaces and Chess / product and engineering:** What remains open when paid access ends while a person is choosing a second space or the next rated puzzle? An already-started puzzle may finish; no unreviewed modal-dismissal behavior is claimed.
- **Identity + Billing / legal and engineering:** What happens when AccountDeletionConfirmed arrives while provider checkout is already open? Confirm denial, late payment handling, cancellation status and the receipt outcome without restoring the deleted account.
